00005: # Inventory: cream upper cabinet, vertical paper roll and metal holder,
00006: # side-mounted towel ring with draped towel; window and horizontal blinds;
00007: # speckled counter, cropped sink and faucet; water pitcher, gallon jug,
00008: # blender and partly hidden cylindrical canister.
00009: # Hypotheses: arbitrary metre-like scale; cabinet projects from the rear wall.
00010: # Near-to-far: sink/counter, jug/towel, pitcher/blender, cabinet/window/walls.
00011: # Camera and dimensions are independent parameters. Hidden backs are simplified.
00012: CAMERA = (-1.20, -2.35, 1.88)
00013: TARGET = (-0.12, 0.20, 1.51)

00021: scene = Scene("Kitchen corner")
00022: 
00023: def add(parent, key, node):
00024:     return parent.include(key, node)
00025: 
00026: def ellipse(parent, key, center, radii, color, opacity=1):
00027:     n = ball(1, center=center, color=color, opacity=opacity)
00028:     n.resize(radii, pivot=center)
00029:     return add(parent, key, n)
00030: 
00031: def tube(parent, key, points, radius, color):
00032:     return add(parent, key, sweep_tube(points=points, radius=radius, color=color))
00033: 
00034: def loop_points(center, r1, r2, plane="xz", count=24):
00035:     pts = []
00036:     for i in range(count + 1):
00037:         a = 2 * math.pi * i / count
00038:         if plane == "xz":
00039:             pts.append((center[0] + r1*math.cos(a), center[1],
00040:                         center[2] + r2*math.sin(a)))
00041:         else:
00042:             pts.append((center[0], center[1] + r1*math.cos(a),
00043:                         center[2] + r2*math.sin(a)))
00044:     return pts
00045:

00036: def ellipse(parent, key, center, radii, color, opacity=1):
00037:     n = ball(1, center=center, color=color, opacity=opacity)
00038:     n.resize(radii, pivot=center)

00041: def tube(parent, key, points, radius, color):

00044: def loop_points(center, r1, r2, plane="xz", count=24):
00045:     pts = []
00046:     for i in range(count + 1):
00047:         a = 2 * math.pi * i / count
00048:         if plane == "xz":
00049:             pts.append((center[0] + r1*math.cos(a), center[1],
00050:                         center[2] + r2*math.sin(a)))
00051:         else:
00052:             pts.append((center[0], center[1] + r1*math.cos(a),
00053:                         center[2] + r2*math.sin(a)))
00054:     return pts

00005: # Inventory: cream wall cabinet, paper roll with brackets, towel ring and towel,
00006: # window and horizontal blinds, granite counter, cropped sink and faucet,

00009: # Far anchors: cabinet underside, window jambs, pitcher and rear wall.
00010: # Jug occludes pitcher; towel occludes canister. Hidden geometry is hypothetical.

00012: # Revision family: CAMERA.
00013: # Visual observation: step 2 shows the outside of the window filling the left
00014: # half, hiding towel, faucet and blender. The paper roll and jug remain visible.
00015: # Hypothesis: the camera crossed the window plane, producing architectural
00016: # occlusion; this is not evidence that those objects are missing.
00017: # Move camera inside the window plane and closer to the counter, with a wider
00018: # lens to retain cabinet, window and counter anchors in the portrait frame.
00019: # Affected module: camera only. All assemblies, fittings, support heights,
00020: # geometry and lighting remain as before. Risk: perspective enlargement near
00021: # the foreground sink. Runtime fact: both previous builds executed successfully.

00033: def add(parent, key, node):
00034:     return parent.include(key, node)
00035: 
00036: def ellipse(parent, key, center, radii, color, opacity=1):
00037:     n = ball(1, center=center, color=color, opacity=opacity)
00038:     n.resize(radii, pivot=center)
00039:     return add(parent, key, n)
00040: 
00041: def tube(parent, key, points, radius, color):
00042:     return add(parent, key, sweep_tube(points=points, radius=radius, color=color))
00043: 
00044: def loop_points(center, r1, r2, plane="xz", count=24):
00045:     pts = []
00046:     for i in range(count + 1):
00047:         a = 2 * math.pi * i / count
00048:         if plane == "xz":
00049:             pts.append((center[0]+r1*math.cos(a), center[1],
00050:                         center[2]+r2*math.sin(a)))
00051:         else:
00052:             pts.append((center[0], center[1]+r1*math.cos(a),
00053:                         center[2]+r2*math.sin(a)))
00054:     return pts
00055:
